Hi. Well, I guess what finally got me here is that work is becoming a real problem. I'm an electrician, and lately, I just... I can't keep my focus on one estimate or code form anymore. 

It's like every little thing pulls me off track. I'll be trying to work, and I'll hear a tool noise, or my phone will buzz with a text, or I'll suddenly get some new idea about how to reorganize the workshop, and I just drop what I'm doing. Because of it, my jobs are starting to get mixed up, and I really can't afford for that to keep happening.

I'll be trying to work, and I'll hear a tool noise, or my phone will buzz with a text, or I'll suddenly get some new idea about how to reorganize the workshop, and I just drop what I'm doing. I mean... it's mainly just at work where it's actually causing a problem. \n\nWhen I'm off the clock, I don't really have strict tasks or forms I have to get through, so if I bounce around doing different things around the house, it doesn't really matter as much. So, yeah, it's definitely causing some problems.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel restless or fidgety, like you can't sit still?\n\nPatient: Yeah, definitely. I've just been going nonstop lately. I'm constantly bouncing from job sites to putting together bids, or I'm back in the workshop rewiring test panels. I mean, I might get distracted and start doing something else, like I said, but I wouldn't say I'm doing things that are risky or impulsive. Just... getting sidetracked, mostly. I try to think things through, especially when it comes to the electrical work, because you kind of have to, you know? So, no, I don't really feel like I act on impulse.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling anxious or stressed about your work or anything else lately?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I mean, obviously it's a little stressful that I'm getting behind on my estimates and messing up the paperwork, but... I wouldn't say I feel anxious. I'm not sitting around worrying all the time or feeling stressed out in general. It's just the distraction thing that's the problem.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced any significant symptoms or issues besides the difficulty with focusing at work?\n\nPatient: No, not really. Um, it's honestly just the trouble focusing at work. That's the only thing that's really been bothering me. Everything else is pretty much normal, I guess. I haven't really noticed any other issues.\n\nDoctor: Did you have trouble focusing or getting distracted when you were a kid?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think so. I mean, I did fine in school, graduated, went to community college for a bit before I started my apprenticeship. I never really had issues paying attention in class or anything like that back then. This whole getting sidetracked thing is just... it's just happening lately.\n\nDoctor: Have you been experiencing these distractions and focus issues at work for more than 6 months?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I'd say it's been going on for a little over six months. It's been building up for about seven months now. That's right around the time my business partner and I took on the mortgage for our new workshop and all the equipment. It's just been getting worse since then.\n\nDoctor: Are you experiencing any problems in your personal relationships or social life due to your difficulty with focusing?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it's definitely spilling over. At work, um, clients have actually started complaining about missed details and me mixing up the scheduling. Even my apprentices... they just don't really trust my direction the way they used to. \n\nAnd at home, well, it's causing a lot of friction. The mortgage and the business are just turning into constant arguments with my wife. She feels like I'm putting everything we've built at risk because I can't keep my head straight. So, yeah, it's definitely causing some problems.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel restless or fidgety, like you can't sit still?\n\nPatient: Yeah, definitely. I've just been going nonstop lately. I'm constantly bouncing from job sites to putting together bids, or I'm back in the workshop rewiring test panels. I keep starting these new home-automation ideas, too, usually before the last project is even finished. It just feels like sitting still is a waste of time right now. I just have to keep moving.\n\nDoctor: Are you having trouble completing tasks or meeting deadlines in your personal life, outside of work?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I mean, outside of the business, I don't exactly have strict deadlines to meet. It's mostly just stuff around the house. Like I said, I start those home-automation projects and maybe I bounce around between them a bit, but I wouldn't say I'm having trouble finishing things in my personal life. The missed details and scheduling issues are pretty much all on the work side of things.\n\nDoctor: Would you say your difficulty with focusing and getting distracted is affecting your overall quality of life?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it really is. I mean, like I mentioned a minute ago, it's hitting both my work and my home life pretty hard. \n\nGetting those complaints from clients about mixed-up schedules and missed details... it's stressful. And having my own apprentices lose trust in my direction is tough to deal with. Then I go home, and it's just constant arguments with my wife about the business and the mortgage. She really feels like I'm putting everything we've built at risk because my head is all over the place. So, yeah, I'd definitely say it's taking a toll on my quality of life.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever been diagnosed with or treated for ADHD or any other mental health condition?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't. I mean, I've never been diagnosed with ADHD or... or anything else, really. I haven't ever seen a doctor for any kind of mental health stuff before this.\n\nDoctor: Are you experiencing these distractions and difficulties with focus in situations where you're really interested in what you're doing?\n\nPatient: No, not really. If it's something I'm actually really interested in, I don't seem to have that problem. My focus is usually fine then. It's mostly just the everyday work tasks and the routine stuff where my mind just kind of... goes everywhere else.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that your difficulties with focusing and getting distracted are significantly impacting your ability to function effectively in your daily life?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it really is. I know I kind of just said this, but it's... it's definitely messing with my ability to function. At work, clients are actually complaining now because I'm missing details and mixing up the schedule. And my apprentices, they just don't trust my direction like they used to, which makes the job a lot harder. \n\nAnd then at home, it's just... it's constant arguments with my wife. We fight about the business, we fight about the mortgage. She feels like I'm putting everything we've built at risk because I just can't keep my head straight. So, yeah, it's definitely impacting my daily life a lot.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
